# Founder PCB first start - 2026-11-12

Firmware target: `grova_founder_s3` on branch `codex/founder-pcb-bringup`.
Current acceptance: Founder application installed on the new S3 board
(MAC `7c:4f:ad:b7:f0:60`). Real phone Wi-Fi onboarding, saved-network reconnect,
RGB sequence/status and short battery-backed RTC retention were checked on
2026-11-12. See the dated observations below for results and limitations.

**Open:** WAGO output connectors are not soldered yet; no physical output/load
acceptance was performed. Sensors are not assembled. SSD1317 modules did not
ACK; SSD1306 replacement displayed correctly with rotation 2, but display and
encoder integration are deferred. Power-path and manual BOOT/RESET issues below
remain unresolved. This is the new S3 board, never Cube 001 or 002.

## 1. Power and boot

Check that the delivered board matches the versioned schematic and N16R8 module.
Use the schematic power selection: H2 selects USB vs external 5V supply;
do not assume all 12V output functions are powered from USB alone.

Known USB-only power issue: U10 VIN is supplied from H2 pin 2, but R24 feeds its
EN from the separate +5V rail generated by the 12V converter. The reported
measurements were USB +5V at H2 pin 1, external +5V at pin 3 = 0V, 3V3 = 0V and
U10 EN = 0V. The user reported that joining all three H2 contacts lights the
board; this ties USB VBUS to the converter output and is not an accepted power
selection or verified long-term workaround. Never attach external power with
that three-contact bridge. Correct H2 selection is 1-2 for USB, 2-3 for external;
USB-only operation additionally needs the EN issue resolved. Proposed rework is
to move R24's supply end from +5V to the selected supply at H2 pin 2, retaining
the 10k resistor. No rework or successful rail/USB acceptance is confirmed here.

Verify the GPIO46/RTC_INT/R58 issue with the actual PCB before relying on
manual BOOT/RESET entry. Normal flash boot ignores GPIO46 when GPIO0 is HIGH;
manual joint USB/UART download requires both LOW. Automatic USB Serial/JTAG
entry is a separate path and does not prove manual recovery works. No code
change can repair the initial reset-time strap. No hardware rework is assumed.

## 2. First flash

From the firmware repository, after confirming the new board's actual COM port:

```powershell
C:\Users\Kai\.platformio\penv\Scripts\pio.exe device list
C:\Users\Kai\.platformio\penv\Scripts\pio.exe run -e grova_founder_s3 -t upload --upload-port COM7
C:\Users\Kai\.platformio\penv\Scripts\pio.exe device monitor --port COM7 --baud 115200
```

COM7 is an example, not a detected device. No upload has been performed.
The normal upload writes boot components and the app with their correct offsets.
An optional prebuilt first-flash package is generated by
`python tools/package_founder.py`; consult its README and manifest before use.
Do not erase flash or reuse the merged first-flash image for ordinary updates
of a configured board: that would discard provisioning data.

## 3. OLED and Wi-Fi

Use the dedicated system OLED connector (root bus, SDA GPIO8/SCL GPIO9), not a
mux expansion port. Check its pin order GND/VCC/SDA/SCL against the module.
The attached module is specified as SSD1317, 64x128. The firmware supports
rotation and COM offset; exact panel mapping requires a real visual test.
The module must include its own required display power circuitry.

After the short GROVA welcome animation, the first start remains in Rest. OLED should show `GROVA-<12 hex MAC digits>`,
the setup key and AP IP. Connect the phone to that protected WLAN, open the
shown IP, enter the key, scan and save the home WLAN. Credentials are committed
only after a successful connection test. Open the new LAN IP for board controls.

If the OLED stays blank, use USB console:

```text
status
setup
display 0 80
```

`setup` explicitly prints the same AP credentials so OLED failure does not
block phone setup. `display <rotation> <offset>` changes only display settings;
rotation is 0..3, offset 0..95. The default offset 80 is provisional from a
64-MUX reference panel, not confirmation of the exact module layout.

## 4. Peripheral checks

- `status`: confirm 16 MB flash and 8 MB PSRAM, output readiness, mux and RTC.
- Connect the selected supported climate sensor on one mux port, then inspect
  its channel/address and temperature/humidity. Do not guess connector order.
- After Internet/NTP synchronization, check RV-3032 valid time and write status;
  verify time retention with the real battery and a complete power interruption.
- Outputs remain zero in Rest. Release Rest deliberately, then test each neutral
  switch and each analogue setpoint individually with a meter before a load.
- In Rest, use Belegung bearbeiten** to occupy the fixed
  connector and select its real load profile. Voltage follows the connector. Follow [the device catalog](founder-equipment.md).
  Pump profiles have a 10-minute boot lock and a maximum 10-second pulse. Measure
  actual cutoff and confirm reset/Rest/setup/OTA leave the load off.
- Test both fan PWM/tacho paths with actual connected fans; verify fault and
  zero-command behaviour of those fans rather than assuming zero means stopped.
- Hold CONFIG five seconds: Rest and setup recovery should activate without
  deleting the last validated WLAN. Confirm recovery on phone and OLED/USB.

Record actual findings, including any failed step. Software tests do not close
these physical checks. ADC/OneWire/RS485/safety contact functions and the future
automation engine are not enabled by this first-start software.

## Observed ROM bring-up, 2026-11-12

User confirmed USB-only supply with all three H2 contacts bridged (temporary,
not an accepted dual-supply configuration; no external supply may be connected
with that bridge). Windows now enumerates Espressif VID 303A / PID 1001 as COM7
and USB JTAG/serial debug, repeatedly disconnecting during normal ROM boot.
Passive serial capture with DTR/RTS initially deasserted showed:

```text
ESP-ROM:esp32s3-20210327
boot:0xc (SPI_FAST_FLASH_BOOT)
invalid header: 0xffffffff
rst:0x7 (TG0WDT_SYS_RST)
```

ROM cannot read a valid bootloader header; a never-programmed module is the
leading explanation, not proof that the whole flash is erased. The prepared
GROVA firmware has not been uploaded.

A read-only `esptool 4.11.0 --before usb_reset --after no_reset --no-stub flash_id`
succeeded on COM7. Reported ESP32-S3 QFN56 rev 0.2, embedded PSRAM 8 MB AP_3v3,
40 MHz crystal, 16 MB quad flash and 3.3 V flash eFuse setting; MAC
`7c:4f:ad:b7:f0:60`. The diagnostic entered USB Serial/JTAG ROM download mode and
left the chip there. It did not write/erase flash, load the RAM flasher stub or
change eFuses. This demonstrates automatic USB entry on this board; manual
BOOT/RESET recovery with the GPIO46/R58 pull-up is still not validated.
Rail measurements, power-path correction, firmware first flash and peripheral
acceptance remain open.


## First application flash, 2026-11-12

Successfully flashed the same COM7 / MAC `7c:4f:ad:b7:f0:60` board with
`grova_founder_s3`, based on 9343a3f plus the local GROVA inverted-V A glyph
change. Founder host tests and S3 build passed. All four written components
passed esptool hash verification. No outputs were activated.

The initial explicit QIO bootloader-header override caused ROM boot loops
(`ets_loader.c 78`, TG0 watchdog). Rewriting only the bootloader with DIO fixed
startup. PlatformIO itself maps QIO to DIO for this header; the packaging script
now matches that behaviour. Application memory remains qio_opi.

Live USB `status` confirmed application uptime, settings_ok/outputs_ready true,
16 MB flash, approximately 8 MB PSRAM, Rest enabled, all output setpoints zero,
and both native fan ports disabled at zero. TCA9548A mux and RV-3032 RTC are
present. RTC time is not yet valid/synchronized. OLED is NOT detected; no climate
sensor is detected and WLAN is not connected. The GROVA boot wordmark uses an
inverted V for A, but the panel animation is not physically verified.

This supersedes the pre-flash state above. Physical rail measurements, corrected
power selection, OLED, Wi-Fi setup, peripheral/load tests and manual BOOT/RESET
recovery remain open. USB-only with three H2 pins bridged remains temporary;
never add external power with that bridge.


### OLED connection diagnostic, 2026-11-12

User reports 3.3 V directly at the OLED, module order GND/VCC/SCL/SDA,
and individually routed SCL/SDA to the direct system bus before the mux.
Added USB `i2c` diagnostic, allowed only in Rest; successfully built and flashed
application only at 0x10000 (hash verified). Live scan found only root 0x51 and
0x70, no other ACK across non-reserved addresses 0x08..0x77 or any mux port.
Status after scan: uptime 30 s, Rest enabled, OLED absent. This is missing I2C
acknowledgement, not merely a blank image or row-offset setting. OLED wiring,
signal levels, interface mode and module health remain to be checked.


### Replacement OLED / selectable profile, 2026-11-12

Replacement display ACKs at root 0x3C; OLED initialization transactions succeed,
but user reports a black panel. User identifies GM009605V4.3, same display type
as the old PCB. The old firmware uses SSD1306 128x64 with SWITCHCAPVCC.
Added explicit USB `panel ssd1306` / `panel ssd1317` selection (Rest required),
stored atomically with rotation/offset in the existing panel record. SSD1306
uses offset 0 and charge pump; SSD1317 retains its default offset 80. There is
no automatic controller inference from an ACK at 0x3C. Status reports the selected
profile, not hardware identity. Existing settings without the new flag retain
SSD1317. Host tests (including profile persistence/offset validation) and S3
build passed. Update attempt could not open COM7; subsequent enumeration showed
only COM1. SSD1306 update is therefore PREPARED, NOT INSTALLED. Reconnect the
identified new board, flash application, select `panel ssd1306`, then verify image
visually; visible output is not yet confirmed.

### WLAN disconnect diagnostics installed, 2026-11-12

Real trial failed with connection_failed; user selected an SSID from the scan.
Added ESP STA disconnect-reason capture (atomic event-to-loop handoff), exposed
non-secret reason in setup status and USB wifi diagnostic, with reason-specific
UI text. Deliberate disconnect reason 8 is ignored. Lifecycle/Wi-Fi host tests,
connection browser tests and S3 build passed; application flashed with hash
verification. Actual reason requires a new user trial; no root cause confirmed.


### Legacy Wi-Fi comparison, 2026-11-12

Compared 5c15d7f (pre-onboarding) and grova-core-v1-final Wi-Fi source. The
original connects directly with WiFi.begin in STA mode; onboarding additionally
disconnects immediately before begin and operates AP+STA during trials. Founder
now omits the extra disconnect and captures begin return value for USB wifi
diagnostics. No router settings or credentials were changed. Founder host tests
and S3 build passed; flashed with hash verification. Prior actual failed trial
reported reason 0 (no captured non-deliberate disconnect), so the extra
disconnect is a hypothesis, NOT a confirmed cause. A new real trial is needed.


### Confirmed onboarding timeout defect fixed, 2026-11-12

USB monitoring of a real attempt jumped from setup to failed with begin=6 and
reason=0. Code review found the loop sampled `now` before queued connectTo(),
which set attemptAt later. Unsigned now-attemptAt underflow could trigger the
30-second trial timeout immediately. The loop now samples time after radio
startup; fallback reconnect timing also uses a fresh millis() value.

Added radio_start_elapsed scenario with 25 ms consumed inside WiFi.begin.
The test FAILS against the old timestamp ordering and PASSES with the fix,
including the exact 30-second deadline. All 25 lifecycle and 17 Wi-Fi scenarios,
HTTP tests and the S3 build passed. Flashed application with hash verification.
This supersedes the earlier unconfirmed extra-disconnect hypothesis as the
reproduced software defect. Real successful home-WLAN connection remains open.
Application SHA-256: `5b5a22f5dc1f1c991308a166420ec16f4f4168c17b1f3c9045f7f3f7263290f1`.

This confirms successful real association/DHCP and credential persistence

and S3 build passed. Application flashed with hash verification. Visual RGB
confirmation is still pending. No actuator commands were sent.

### RTC battery power-interruption check, 2026-11-12

Following instructions to remove all main supplies for about 30 seconds while
keeping the battery connected, user reported board reconnected. Live status
at uptime 12 s: RV-3032 present/valid/backup_configured/used_for_boot true;
last_write_ok true, NTP synchronized, WLAN reconnected, Rest enabled, healthy.
The latched used_for_boot flag confirms valid RTC time was used before later
NTP synchronization. Short interruption retention is supported by this result,
conditional on the instructed complete supply removal. No precision/drift or
long-duration battery endurance measurement was performed.
